| 2026-09-08 | The row was literally "first ten series", the thing the ruling names | [CONFIRMED, read from the source] `MarketComparativeRibbon.tsx` collected every populated series in registry order and took `rows.slice(0, MAX_METRICS)` with `MAX_METRICS = 10`, and its header read shown-of-total over SERIES. Selection is now `selectHeadlineSeries` (`src/lib/market/headline-series-select.mjs`) over families declared in `src/lib/market/series-family.mjs`, and the component renders the selection it is handed without counting anything itself. | Operator ruling 2026-09-08 |

A column is only the better home if something populates it, and nothing does: no producer writes a family, and a family is a display decision rather than an observation any source publishes, so the rule would still have to be written down somewhere to populate the column, leaving two homes for one decision. Membership is data (a list of exact `series_key` values), and an unclaimed series becomes its own family with its class taken from its producer's registry entry, so a new series gets a family the day it lands with nobody editing anything. | Lane, on the brief's own two options |
| 2026-09-08 | Today's live row is NOT the operator's stated row, and is not forced to be | [CONFIRMED against the live database, project kwrsbpiseruzbfwjpvsp, read-only] His row is Diesel, Euro-super 95, HFO, Residual fuel oil, EUR/USD. His own rules over live data give Diesel, Euro-Super 95, Heavy fuel oil 3.5%S, Residual fuel oil 1%S, Jet fuel. Two independent causes, both in the data rather than in the rules. First, rule 4: all four ECB FX reference rates hold exactly ONE observation each (loaded 2026-09-04), so none carries a delta and the FX family has no eligible member at all, which is the same state artboard 04 itself draws on that card, a dash and a BACKFILL chip. Second, rule 2 plus rule 3: EIA v2 landed six fuel and crude series on 2026-09-04 with 454 to 455 observations each, so there are twelve fuel series today rather than four, and fuels fill all five slots before FX is reached. The fifth card is jet fuel, an air-freight fuel, not a duplicate diesel, because the diesel and gasoline families each fold their EIA quote onto the EU card. | Lane; reported for a ruling, not resolved by the lane |

The ruling is explicit and later: "header count is 'N of 16' where N is distinct families shown", "today that reads five". The ruling wins over the image on this one value, and only this one; the denominator, 16, is the same in both. | Operator ruling 2026-09-08, over dc.html p4 |
| 2026-09-08 | Rule 4 is implemented as a delta test, not an observation count | [CONFIRMED, tested] "One observation, no delta yet" is `series-deltas.mjs`'s message for a single point, but a two-point series can also produce no delta: both points inside the same window, a null value on either side, or a unit or currency change between them, which that module refuses to compare across on purpose. All four are the same fact for the reader, so the test is the outcome: at least one of 1w, 1m, YoY carries a numeric change. A change whose PERCENT is undefined because the prior value was zero still counts, since the movement is known and only its formatting is in question. | Lane, rule 14 |

## FOLD 63 (2026-09-08)

Train `train/wave63-2026-09-08`, folded onto `train/wave62-2026-09-08` (`605413d9`) from five lanes
that each branched from `train/wave61-2026-09-08` (`9f549170`): `regopscope`, `communitynav2`,
`market63`, `seriesfamily`, `sharedreport`. The union of their deviations is the five sections above
this one, unchanged. What follows is what the FOLD itself decided, which is every case where two
lanes disagreed, where a lane's work met a wave-62 shared part it had never seen, or where a gate
found something no lane had.

| Date | Page / deviation | Detail | Ruled by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-09-08 | THE HEADLINE SERIES ROW: selection is seriesfamily's, geometry is market63's, and there is ONE of each | [CONFIRMED, both halves measured] Lanes `market63` and `seriesfamily` both rewrote `/market`'s HEADLINE SERIES row from different sources. market63 built one row of five compact cards off artboard 04, measured in chromium. seriesfamily implements the operator's LATER ruling, which says WHICH series the row contains. The later ruling supersedes, so the split is exact: `selectHeadlineSeries` decides the families and their order and the head count reads N distinct families of M observed series (seriesfamily); the row's geometry and type are market63's (`grid-auto-flow: column`, `calc((100% - 40px) / 5)`, no sparkline, no 1m or YoY row, Anton 17px value with the delta inline). The OVERFLOW is market63's because overflow is geometry: families past the ruling's cap of five continue the SAME row into the horizontal scroller. seriesfamily's `MoreBelowDisclosure` and market63's `MAX_METRICS = 10` are both deleted; the cap is `HEADLINE_VISIBLE_CAP` in `headline-series-select.mjs`, the ruling's own five. One selection path, one row, one cap (rule 13). | The operator's later ruling; the coordinator's fold rule |
| 2026-09-08 | The compose-04 FIXTURE is seriesfamily's four real ECB keys, not market63's twelve invented ones, and that is a correctness constraint rather than a preference | [CONFIRMED by reading `series-family.mjs:148-153`] Family membership is by EXACT `series_key`. The declared FX family names `ecb-fx:eur-usd`, `-gbp`, `-cny`, `-jpy` and no others. market63's fixture keys (`ecb-fx:eurusd`, `ecb-fx:eurgbp`, ...) are claimed by no family, so under the ruling each would resolve to a family of its own and the row would render twelve singleton FX cards with no "+3 rates" fold anywhere, which is the exact shape the ruling exists to forbid. market63's reason for twelve (a cap that overflows, so the assertion is not vacuous) is answered rather than dropped: the cap and the overflow ORDER are proven exhaustively by attack in `market-headline-series-select.test.mjs`, which the npmtest glob runs, and the DOM spec proves one card per selected family. | Rule 13; rule 15 |

**A gate note the operator should have, because the brief this fold was given says the opposite.**
The fold was told the base was green on every gate. Measured on a clean checkout of `605413d9`:
`npx tsc --noEmit` FAILS (TS1117), `next build --webpack` FAILS at the TypeScript step, the fitness
runner reports 2 F42 violations, `npm run audit:design` reports 1 MISMATCH, the CI npmtest glob has
15 failing assertions, and the rendering guard reports 221 failures. The likely cause is that those
runs were made in `/root/work/lanes/train62`, whose working tree carries substantial uncommitted
work including a fix for the duplicate key. Every one of the six is green or strictly improved on
this fold; the rendering guard is the one that still fails, and its failure set is a strict SUBSET
of the base's.
